**[00:34:56.850] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000177)  
Dalawa po ito sa nabigyan din po namin ng DBM.

**[00:35:01.890] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000178)  
Kung titignan po natin ang detalye,

**[00:35:05.570] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000179)  
sa rail project po, yung pre-propose po namin 7 projects for foreign assisted projects.

**[00:35:15.230] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000180)  
Apat lang po ang nabigyan ng DBM.

**[00:35:17.490] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000181)  
So ito po yung LRT Line 1 Cavite Extension,

**[00:35:21.750] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000182)  
the Metro Manila Subway,

**[00:35:23.490] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000183)  
the MRT Line 3 Rehab Project at the North-South Commuter Railway System.

**[00:35:30.030] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000184)  
So ito po yung nabigyan nila out of the 7 projects na amin pong iprinopose.

**[00:35:36.070] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000185)  
Hindi po nabigyan yung sa Mindanao Railway,

**[00:35:39.270] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000186)  
MRT Line 4 at sa PNR South Long Haul Project.

**[00:35:44.790] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000187)  
Sa railway sector pa rin po, locally funded projects,

**[00:35:49.290] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000188)  
ang prinopose po namin ay 13 projects.

**[00:35:52.330] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000189)  
So balit,

**[00:35:53.310] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000190)  
dalawa lang po ang binigay ng DBM.

**[00:35:56.770] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000191)  
So yung aming pong mga locally projects for railway as enumerated in our presentation,

**[00:36:04.090] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000192)  
hindi po naibigay ng DBM.

**[00:36:06.470] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000193)  
Totaling to 97.9 billion.

**[00:36:11.490] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000194)  
Sa road transport po,

**[00:36:13.430] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000195)  
ang prinopose po namin for foreign assisted ay dalawa.

**[00:36:17.310] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000196)  
Luckily po, nabigyan naman po ng allocation sa NEP

**[00:36:21.630] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000197)  
yung dalawang foreign assisted projects.

**[00:36:23.310] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000198)  
So balit, kung titignan po yung amount,

**[00:36:27.730] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000199)  
ang aming pong proposal was 19 billion,

**[00:36:31.310] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000200)  
pero ang ibinigay lang po nila ay 15 billion, worth 15 billion.

**[00:36:36.750] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000201)  
So may kakulangan pong 4.7 billion compared to our proposed requirement.

**[00:36:44.410] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000202)  
On the locally funded projects for road transport,

**[00:36:48.430] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000203)  
ang proposal po namin ay labing pitong project.

**[00:36:53.310] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000208)  
So balit, binigyan lang po kami ng allocation for 5 projects

**[00:36:56.610] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000209)  
na nandito po, nakalista po sa aming presentation.

**[00:37:02.270] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000210)  
Ngayon din po, yung mga projects po,

**[00:37:04.610] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000211)  
totaling 12 items na hindi po nabigyan ng DBM ng allocation.

**[00:37:09.990] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000212)  
Ang total amount po ay 12,979,000.

**[00:37:15.330] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000213)  
Hindi po nabigyan.

**[00:37:17.390] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000214)  
Sa aviation sector po,

**[00:37:19.130] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000215)  
sa foreign assisted po, medyo maswerte din po kami.

**[00:37:23.310] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000216)  
Sa pagkat yung dalawang proposal po namin for foreign assisted

**[00:37:27.030] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000217)  
ay naibigay naman po ng DBM.

**[00:37:30.110] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000218)  
So balit, kung titignan po yung amount,

**[00:37:33.110] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000219)  
again, ang proposal po namin was 3.8 billion for foreign assisted projects.

**[00:37:39.390] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000220)  
Ang naibigay lang po sa amin is halos kalahati, 1.9 billion.

**[00:37:44.910] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000221)  
So makikita po natin na medyo kulang pa rin po

**[00:37:50.630] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000222)  
than the requirement yung naibigay ng DBM.

**[00:37:53.310] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000223)  
Sa aviation sector na locally funded projects,

**[00:37:57.250] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000224)  
ang aming pong proposal is 40 projects for zero.

**[00:38:01.970] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000225)  
Ang naibigay lang po nila ay para sa walong proyekto.

**[00:38:06.670] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000226)  
Totaling 3.9 billion out of our total proposal of 19.30 billion.

**[00:38:15.250] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000227)  
So makikita po din sa aming presentation,

**[00:38:18.270] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000228)  
yung list po ng mga hindi nabigyan ng DBM

**[00:38:22.030] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000229)  
out of our total proposal of 19.30 billion.

**[00:38:23.110] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000230)  
So that's the total of our submitted proposal.

**[00:38:26.090] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000231)  
Sa maritime po, sa foreign assisted projects,

**[00:38:29.910] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000232)  
medyo maganda rin po in terms of ano,

**[00:38:32.830] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000233)  
because yung apat na projects na pinopost po namin

**[00:38:37.430] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000234)  
ay naibigay din po ng DBM.

**[00:38:40.050] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000235)  
So balit, looking at the amount,

**[00:38:42.770] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000236)  
our total proposal is 22.7 billion,

**[00:38:46.850] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000237)

**[00:38:50.950] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000238)  
Sa locally funded projects,

**[00:38:53.110] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000239)  
po ng maritime, medyo malungkot po ang sitwasyon

**[00:38:57.190] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000240)  
because out of the 91 projects we have proposed,

**[00:39:01.710] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000241)  
isa lang po ang binigay ng DBM.

**[00:39:04.490] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000242)  
At yun po yung aming, what's this,

**[00:39:09.230] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000243)  
lump sum amount para po sa maritime.

**[00:39:11.950] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000244)  
So mga projects po, 90 items po,

**[00:39:15.430] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000245)  
yung hindi nila nabigyan ng alokasyon.

**[00:39:18.450] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000246)  
So nakalista rin po yung mga projects

**[00:39:21.530] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000247)  
na hindi po nabigyan ng alokasyon.

**[00:39:23.110] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000248)  
So ito po yung ano namin sa IPIF at saka sa FAST-INPRA.

**[00:39:36.260] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000249)  
So ang susunod po is yung para sa right-of-way allocation.

**[00:39:40.860] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000250)  
Sa aming pong right-of-way,

**[00:39:43.020] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000251)  
ang total proposal po namin is 98.5 billion.

**[00:39:48.760] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000252)  
So balit, ang naibigay lang po sa amin is 2.79 billion.

**[00:39:53.560] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000253)  
So,

**[00:39:55.960] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000254)  
pero yung pong 2.79 billion na yun

**[00:39:59.060] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000255)  
is not even enough

**[00:40:00.920] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000256)  
for the requirement of the NSCR projects.

**[00:40:05.480] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000257)  
So yung mga ibang items po

is talaga pong medyo

**[00:40:08.900] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000259)  
hindi po namin, hindi po nabigyan ng alokasyon

**[00:40:16.580] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000261)  
So,

**[00:40:17.100] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000262)  
eto naman po, just to end,

ito po yung budgetary support to government corporations

So, para po sa LRTA at sa PNR,

ito po yung mga korporasyon na attached sa DOTR,

totaling 1.2 billion po.

**[00:40:38.140] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000269)  
Marami pong salamat.

**[00:41:38.680] AVP Narration** (asr-000289)  
The Department of Transportation

**[00:41:40.320] AVP Narration** (asr-000290)  
has been relentless in achieving significant strides

**[00:41:43.200] AVP Narration** (asr-000291)  
to improve the country's transport system.

**[00:41:44.080] AVP Narration** (asr-000292)  
to improve the country's public transport system.

**[00:41:46.280] AVP Narration** (asr-000293)  
President Ferdinand Marcos Jr.

**[00:41:48.060] AVP Narration** (asr-000294)  
has ordered the DOTR

**[00:41:49.420] AVP Narration** (asr-000295)  
to expedite the construction of critical

**[00:41:51.400] AVP Narration** (asr-000296)  
big-ticket transport infrastructure projects

**[00:41:54.000] AVP Narration** (asr-000297)  
and initiate commuter-focused programs

**[00:41:56.460] AVP Narration** (asr-000298)  
to ease the daily commute of Filipinos.

**[00:41:59.500] AVP Narration** (asr-000299)  
Among the commuter-centric initiatives

**[00:42:01.340] AVP Narration** (asr-000300)  
the DOTR has implemented

**[00:42:02.860] AVP Narration** (asr-000301)  
were the 50% discounts for senior citizens,

**[00:42:06.220] AVP Narration** (asr-000302)  
students and persons with disabilities,

**[00:42:09.100] AVP Narration** (asr-000303)  
the 1 plus 3 Pamilya Pass promo,

**[00:42:12.100] AVP Narration** (asr-000304)  
and 12 Days of Christmas

**[00:42:13.320] AVP Narration** (asr-000305)  
Libring sa Kaibigan.

**[00:42:14.080] AVP Narration** (asr-000306)  
The program across major rail lines

**[00:42:15.820] AVP Narration** (asr-000307)  
to encourage commuters and motorists

**[00:42:17.980] AVP Narration** (asr-000308)  
to public transport

**[00:42:19.300] AVP Narration** (asr-000309)  
and at the same time

**[00:42:20.680] AVP Narration** (asr-000310)  
helping them save on transport costs.

**[00:42:23.400] AVP Narration** (asr-000311)  
The government also relaunched

**[00:42:24.860] AVP Narration** (asr-000312)  
the iconic Love Bus Libring sa Kai initiative

**[00:42:27.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000313)  
through the service contracting program

**[00:42:29.340] AVP Narration** (asr-000314)  
by providing free and reliable

**[00:42:31.260] AVP Narration** (asr-000315)  
public transportation in major urban areas

**[00:42:33.900] AVP Narration** (asr-000316)  
including Metro Manila,

**[00:42:35.980] AVP Narration** (asr-000317)  
Metro Cebu,

**[00:42:37.020] AVP Narration** (asr-000318)  
and Metro Davao.

**[00:42:38.500] AVP Narration** (asr-000319)  
Personalized white beat cards

**[00:42:39.980] AVP Narration** (asr-000320)  
were also distributed

**[00:42:41.120] AVP Narration** (asr-000321)  
with an automatic 50% discount

**[00:42:43.360] AVP Narration** (asr-000322)  
for senior citizens

**[00:42:44.080] AVP Narration** (asr-000323)  
and students riding the MRT3,

**[00:42:48.260] AVP Narration** (asr-000324)  
LRT1,

**[00:42:49.260] AVP Narration** (asr-000325)  
and LRT2 lines.

**[00:42:51.160] AVP Narration** (asr-000326)  
The DOTR continues to improve

**[00:42:53.000] AVP Narration** (asr-000327)  
the MRT3 facilities,

**[00:42:54.840] AVP Narration** (asr-000328)  
ensuring passenger comfort and safety

**[00:42:56.780] AVP Narration** (asr-000329)  
while eliminating long queues

**[00:42:58.720] AVP Narration** (asr-000330)  
and splitting up commuter entry at station.

**[00:43:01.360] AVP Narration** (asr-000331)  
Tension in the Middle East early this year

**[00:43:03.380] AVP Narration** (asr-000332)  
made a heavy impact in many countries

**[00:43:05.380] AVP Narration** (asr-000333)  
including the Philippines.

**[00:43:06.740] AVP Narration** (asr-000334)  
Under uplift,

**[00:43:07.960] AVP Narration** (asr-000335)  
the DOTR and its attached agencies

**[00:43:09.920] AVP Narration** (asr-000336)  
implemented recalibrated support packages

**[00:43:12.340] AVP Narration** (asr-000337)  
that include fair discounts,

**[00:43:14.080] AVP Narration** (asr-000338)  
free rides,

**[00:43:15.080] AVP Narration** (asr-000339)  
fuel subsidy,

**[00:43:16.380] AVP Narration** (asr-000340)  
terminal fee discount,

**[00:43:17.920] AVP Narration** (asr-000341)  
call discount,

**[00:43:19.080] AVP Narration** (asr-000342)  
rebate and transport charges,

**[00:43:21.080] AVP Narration** (asr-000343)  
and service contracting program

**[00:43:22.840] AVP Narration** (asr-000344)  
among others.

**[00:43:23.840] AVP Narration** (asr-000345)  
All this to caution

**[00:43:25.080] AVP Narration** (asr-000346)  
the impact of the Middle East crisis

**[00:43:27.080] AVP Narration** (asr-000347)  
on commuters and motorists.

**[00:43:28.420] AVP Narration** (asr-000348)  
As part of the uplift initiatives,

**[00:43:30.420] AVP Narration** (asr-000349)  
President Marcus Jr. led the launching

**[00:43:32.840] AVP Narration** (asr-000350)  
of the NETS service contracting program

**[00:43:34.840] AVP Narration** (asr-000351)  
in Araneta Center Bus Terminal in Quezon City

**[00:43:37.840] AVP Narration** (asr-000352)  
and the fuel subsidy program

**[00:43:39.580] AVP Narration** (asr-000353)  
in Paranaque Integrated Terminal Exchange.

**[00:43:42.080] AVP Narration** (asr-000354)  
The DOTR likewise continues to improve

**[00:43:43.960] AVP Narration** (asr-000355)  
to implement a 50% fair discount

**[00:43:45.960] AVP Narration** (asr-000356)  
to all passengers with MRT3 and LRT2.

**[00:43:48.960] AVP Narration** (asr-000357)  
In the railway sector,

**[00:43:53.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000358)  
the DOTR is splitting up the construction

**[00:43:55.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000359)  
of mass transit railway systems.

**[00:43:57.540] AVP Narration** (asr-000360)  
One of the biggest is the Metro Manila Subway Project,

**[00:44:00.540] AVP Narration** (asr-000361)  
which is fast taking shape underground

**[00:44:02.540] AVP Narration** (asr-000362)  
from Valenzuela to Naia.

**[00:44:04.540] AVP Narration** (asr-000363)  
Once operational,

**[00:44:06.540] AVP Narration** (asr-000364)  
the 33-kilometer, 17-station Metro Manila Subway Project

**[00:44:10.540] AVP Narration** (asr-000365)  
which will run from Valenzuela City to Naia

**[00:44:13.540] AVP Narration** (asr-000366)  
will reduce travel time from 90 minutes

**[00:44:15.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000367)  
to just 41 minutes

**[00:44:17.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000368)  
and will benefit around 519,000 passengers daily.

**[00:44:21.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000369)  
Last February,

**[00:44:22.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000370)  
President Ferdinand Marcus Jr.

**[00:44:24.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000371)  
led the groundbreaking ceremony

**[00:44:26.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000372)  
of two underground stations and tunnels

**[00:44:28.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000373)  
for the Bonifacio Global City

**[00:44:30.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000374)  
and Kalayaan Avenue Station.

**[00:44:32.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000375)  
It will serve about 200,000 passengers

**[00:44:34.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000376)  
in its opening year.

**[00:44:36.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000377)  
On the other hand,

**[00:44:37.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000378)  
the North-South Commuter Railway's Valenzuela to Malolos segment

**[00:44:40.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000379)  
will be operational this year.

**[00:44:42.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000380)  
Once fully operational,

**[00:44:44.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000381)  
the 147-kilometer rail line

**[00:44:46.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000382)  
can service 800,000 passengers daily,

**[00:44:49.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000383)  
reducing travel time between

**[00:44:50.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000384)  
Clark-Pampanga and Calamba, Laguna

**[00:44:53.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000385)  
from over 4 hours to just 2 hours.

**[00:44:55.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000386)  
Preparations for the NSCR's operations and maintenance

**[00:44:58.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000387)  
are underway.

**[00:45:00.000] AVP Narration** (asr-000388)  
with the public-private partnership market sounding activities being held in different countries.

**[00:45:04.500] AVP Narration** (asr-000389)  
Relocation activities are also ongoing and progressing in Cavite, Laguna, and Pampanga to support communities affected by the said project.

**[00:45:12.260] AVP Narration** (asr-000390)  
Meanwhile, the MRT-7's first 12 stations are expected to be operational by second quarter of next year,

**[00:45:19.200] AVP Narration** (asr-000391)  
connecting North Avenue in Quezon City to Sacred Heart in Caloocan.

**[00:45:23.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000392)  
Once the project is completed, connecting Quezon City all the way to Bulacan will cut travel times from 2 to 3 hours to just 35 minutes

**[00:45:31.720] AVP Narration** (asr-000393)  
and can serve up to 850,000 passengers daily.

**[00:45:36.140] AVP Narration** (asr-000394)  
Existing rail infrastructure is also being rehabilitated to provide an improved and more convenient travel experience for commuters.

**[00:45:44.360] AVP Narration** (asr-000395)  
The rehabilitation of MRT-3's Quezon Avenue station offers clearer and larger wayfinding signs,

**[00:45:50.380] AVP Narration** (asr-000396)  
cleaner and more accessible restrooms,

**[00:45:53.140] AVP Narration** (asr-000397)  
wider passenger spaces,

**[00:45:55.240] AVP Narration** (asr-000398)  
improved ventilation,

**[00:45:57.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000399)  
and platform window louvers and tactile tiles for persons with visual impairment.

**[00:46:01.660] AVP Narration** (asr-000400)  
These upgrades are designed to make commuting easier.

**[00:46:04.820] AVP Narration** (asr-000401)  
As Quezon Avenue station sets the pace for improvements,

**[00:46:08.300] AVP Narration** (asr-000402)  
other MRT-3 stations are set to follow suit as rehabilitation works continue across the line.

**[00:46:14.420] AVP Narration** (asr-000403)  
At the center of Metro Manila's growing rail network is the Common Station,

**[00:46:18.180] AVP Narration** (asr-000404)  
or the Unified Grand Central Station.

**[00:46:21.520] AVP Narration** (asr-000405)  
The connecting LRT-1,

**[00:46:23.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000406)  
MRT-3,

**[00:46:24.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000407)  
and MRT-7

**[00:46:25.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000408)  
will ensure seamless transfers for at least 1.28 million passengers.

**[00:46:31.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000409)  
To the south of Manila,

**[00:46:33.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000410)  
the LRT-1 Cavite Extension is bringing rail service closer to Parañaque, Las Piñas, and Cavite.

**[00:46:39.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000411)  
The 11.7-kilometer extension with eight new stations is expected to reduce Baclaran to Bacoor travel time from around 70 minutes to only 25 minutes.

**[00:46:50.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000412)  
And beyond Metro Manila,

**[00:46:52.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000413)  
the Mindanao Railway project is set to improve the connectivity in Davao Region.

**[00:46:57.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000414)  
Its first phase spans 100.2 kilometers with eight stations,

**[00:47:01.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000415)  
covering Tagum, Davao, and Digos,

**[00:47:04.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000416)  
and is expected to reduce travel time between Tagum and Digos from about three hours to just one hour.

**[00:47:13.660] AVP Narration** (asr-000417)  
To expedite the construction of big-ticket transport projects,

**[00:47:16.660] AVP Narration** (asr-000418)  
the DOTR prioritizes the resolution of right-of-way issues and property acquisition for these projects.

**[00:47:22.660] AVP Narration** (asr-000419)  
Through its specialized right-of-way projects,

**[00:47:23.660] AVP Narration** (asr-000420)  
and its specialized right-of-way and site acquisition office,

**[00:47:26.660] AVP Narration** (asr-000421)  
the land acquisition as of July 2026 for the NSER's Malolos to Clark segment is at 90.73% from 64.93% in 2022,

**[00:47:36.660] AVP Narration** (asr-000422)  
while the Rail Line's Sol-East to Calamba segment is at 64.66% from 27.99% in 2024.

**[00:47:45.660] AVP Narration** (asr-000423)  
Meanwhile, land procurement for the Metro Manila subway is at 90.87% from 31.76% in 2022.

**[00:47:53.660] AVP Narration** (asr-000424)  
While the right-of-way progress for the Mindanao Railway project is currently at 15% from 0.02% in 2022.

**[00:48:05.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000425)  
The DOTR is modernizing the country's road transport,

**[00:48:08.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000426)  
from public utility vehicles,

**[00:48:10.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000427)  
PUV terminals and stops,

**[00:48:12.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000428)  
and active transport infrastructure to encourage commuters to cycle or walk to their destinations.

**[00:48:18.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000429)  
The Transport Agency is pushing for the Active Transport Bike-Share System and Safe Pathways program

**[00:48:23.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000430)  
by building safe pathways, cycleways,

**[00:48:25.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000431)  
and bike-share facilities.

**[00:48:27.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000432)  
Once completed, the 672-kilometer Davao Public Transport Modernization Project

**[00:48:33.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000433)  
can serve up to 800,000 passengers daily,

**[00:48:36.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000434)  
providing commuters with efficient bus service, modern terminals, bus stops, and shelters.

**[00:48:42.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000435)  
The Cebu Bus Rapid Transit is a 17-kilometer bus system,

**[00:48:46.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000436)  
which offers safe and efficient transport for Cebuan.

**[00:48:49.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000437)  
Following the completion of the three stations of Package 1,

**[00:48:52.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000438)  
the DOTr officially launched the Cebu BRT partial

**[00:48:55.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000439)  
operations on March 13, 2026

**[00:48:59.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000440)  
from Fuente, CNU, and CSBT stations.

**[00:49:04.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000441)  
The Ilocos Norte Transport Hub and Parking Building is a multi-level facility in Luwag City,

**[00:49:09.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000442)  
which will serve as a key transport point for commuters

**[00:49:12.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000443)  
traveling within and outside Ilocos Norte.

**[00:49:15.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000444)  
The Taguig City Integrated Terminal Exchange is a multi-model transport hub

**[00:49:19.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000445)  
in Arca South, Taguig City, integrating buses,

**[00:49:22.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000446)  
jeepneys, taxis,

**[00:49:24.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000447)  
the NSCR,

**[00:49:25.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000448)  
and the Metro Manila subway.

**[00:49:27.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000449)  
It is also designed to accommodate about 160,000 passengers and 5,200 PVBs daily.

**[00:49:34.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000450)  
The 30-kilometer EDSA busway project has provided greater ease of travel

**[00:49:38.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000451)  
to over 367 million passengers.

**[00:49:41.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000452)  
Operating 24-7 across 24 stations,

**[00:49:44.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000453)  
it has reduced commuter travel time from Monumento in Caloocan

**[00:49:48.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000454)  
to PITX in Paranaque City from 2.5 to 3 hours down to just 45 to 60 minutes.

**[00:49:54.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000455)  
Meanwhile, the 66.85-kilometer South Luzon Expressway Toll Road 4 project

**[00:49:59.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000456)  
will cut travel time from Santo Tomas, Batangas to Mayao, Quezon by three hours,

**[00:50:04.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000457)  
from the usual 4 hours of travel to just an hour.

**[00:50:08.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000458)  
Package A to C from Santo Tomas, Batangas to Tiyang, Quezon

**[00:50:12.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000459)  
is set to open in December,

**[00:50:14.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000460)  
spreading travel time from 2 hours to only 30 minutes.

**[00:50:17.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000461)  
President Marcus Jr. inaugurated the Cavitex C5 Link Segment 3B last March,

**[00:50:22.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000462)  
which aims to reduce travel time between Paranaque City and Taguig City to just 15 minutes.

**[00:50:27.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000463)  
The DOTr and the Toll Regulatory Board continue to advance the country's toll road infrastructure,

**[00:50:33.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000464)  
streamlining travel and boosting connectivity across key expressways nationwide.

**[00:50:39.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000465)  
President Marcus Jr. inaugurated the Naia Expressway Westbound

**[00:50:43.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000466)  
off-ramp to Terminal 3 on March 27, 2026,

**[00:50:47.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000467)  
further strengthening Naia's connectivity by offering a direct route that bypasses Conjunto.

**[00:50:52.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000468)  
The C5 North Link Expressway project,

**[00:50:57.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000469)  
Segment 8.2 to Section 1A,

**[00:51:00.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000470)  
targeted for inauguration in September 2026,

**[00:51:03.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000471)  
covers the first 2-kilometer portion of the 11.5-kilometer Segment 8.2

**[00:51:08.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000472)  
stretch off to CP Garcia, Katipunan, QC.

**[00:51:12.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000473)  
It shortens the travel time between Mindanao Avenue and Commonwealth Avenue by 35 minutes

**[00:51:17.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000474)  
and be there in just about 10 minutes.

**[00:51:20.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000475)  
President Marcos Jr. inaugurated the One RFID, All Tollways program

**[00:51:21.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000476)  
on October 21, 2025 at SLEX, Laguna,

**[00:51:28.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000477)  
which aims to make electronic and cash tall payment systems work together across expressways

**[00:51:33.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000478)  
for faster and more seamless travel of tall paying motorists.

**[00:51:37.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000479)  
Upon the orders of the President,

**[00:51:42.260] AVP Narration** (asr-000480)  
the DOTR is upgrading and modernizing the country's aviation infrastructure

**[00:51:46.260] AVP Narration** (asr-000481)  
to boost the country's economy, tourism and connectivity.

**[00:51:50.260] AVP Narration** (asr-000482)  
As of July 2026,

**[00:51:52.260] AVP Narration** (asr-000483)  
A total of 83 airport projects nationwide have been successfully completed, improving the country's connectivity and regional progress.

**[00:52:00.340] AVP Narration** (asr-000484)  
In the north, Laoag International Airport has undergone major expansion of its passenger terminal building,

**[00:52:06.200] AVP Narration** (asr-000485)  
increasing its floor space to 7,000 square meters to service wide-bodied aircraft and sit around 980 passengers from the previous 725.

**[00:52:16.020] AVP Narration** (asr-000486)  
In Palawan, dual development initiatives at Busuanga Airport are strengthening and extending runways up to 2,100 meters to accommodate jet operations such as A320,

**[00:52:28.100] AVP Narration** (asr-000487)  
paving the way for stronger tourism growth and easier air travel access.

**[00:52:33.060] AVP Narration** (asr-000488)  
In Albay, the Bicol International Airport's passenger terminal building and site development works are almost 100% physically finished, meeting international standards.

**[00:52:44.720] AVP Narration** (asr-000489)  
Meanwhile, in Western Visayas,

**[00:52:46.020] AVP Narration** (asr-000490)  
in Visayas, PBBM inaugurated last January the new and modernized Antique Airport with its larger passenger terminal, apron, and expanded taxiway.

**[00:52:56.660] AVP Narration** (asr-000491)  
In Bukidnon, the airport's new airside facilities and the 3,600-square-meter terminal are underway to serve jet aircraft operations.

**[00:53:05.520] AVP Narration** (asr-000492)  
Now halfway through completion, the project will seamlessly link Bukidnon and the rest of north-central Mindanao to faster, wider air travel,

**[00:53:13.540] AVP Narration** (asr-000493)  
opening doors for tourism,

**[00:53:16.020] AVP Narration** (asr-000494)  
trade,

**[00:53:16.500] AVP Narration** (asr-000495)  
and new investment in the region.

**[00:53:18.700] AVP Narration** (asr-000496)  
Davao International Airport's expansion includes an 8,400 m2 of terminal space to accommodate up to 2,200 domestic and international passengers.

**[00:53:29.160] AVP Narration** (asr-000497)  
In the country's surfing capital, Siargao, the Siargao airport is expanding its terminal through a modular facility, increasing its seating capacity to 573 and additional check-in counters.

**[00:53:41.500] AVP Narration** (asr-000498)  
Public-private partnerships are also enabling fast track progress and various projects.

**[00:53:46.020] AVP Narration** (asr-000499)  
seamless international and regional travel.

**[00:53:48.720] AVP Narration** (asr-000500)  
At the country's primary gateway, the Ninoy Aquino International Airport underwent major transformation

**[00:53:54.240] AVP Narration** (asr-000501)  
following its turnover to new Naia InfraCorp.

**[00:53:57.380] AVP Narration** (asr-000502)  
Under the NNIC, Naia's annual passenger capacity has doubled from 35 to 62 million.

**[00:54:04.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000503)  
Last December 2025, the President inaugurated the new modern facilities at Naia Terminal 3,

**[00:54:10.640] AVP Narration** (asr-000504)  
activating 78 biometric immigration e-gates, launching the Dignitaries Lounge,

**[00:54:16.900] AVP Narration** (asr-000505)  
and opening the Tambayan and Mazanin food halls for a world-class passenger experience.

**[00:54:22.080] AVP Narration** (asr-000506)  
Following the handover of the Laguindingan Airport to Aboiti's InfraCapital,

**[00:54:26.880] AVP Narration** (asr-000507)  
the airport's current annual passenger capacity of 1.6 million is expected to increase to 3.9 million,

**[00:54:33.940] AVP Narration** (asr-000508)  
with improvements of its airport facilities now underway for long-term efficiency and passenger demand.

**[00:54:39.760] AVP Narration** (asr-000509)  
The ongoing terminal reconfiguration of the Bohol Panglao International Airport

**[00:54:44.640] AVP Narration** (asr-000510)  
will scale up its capacity to 2.5 million passengers,

**[00:54:48.280] AVP Narration** (asr-000511)  
while a new passenger terminal building will increase its further to 3.9 million.

**[00:54:57.000] AVP Narration** (asr-000512)  
Inter-island connectivity has vastly improved as the DOTR and the Philippine Ports Authority

**[00:55:02.040] AVP Narration** (asr-000513)  
continue to modernize the ports to provide efficient movement of people and goods.

**[00:55:06.920] AVP Narration** (asr-000514)  
The Philippine Ports Authority has completed 15 port projects in 2025

**[00:55:11.020] AVP Narration** (asr-000515)  
and six port projects as of June 2026.

**[00:55:13.440] AVP Narration** (asr-000516)  
On April 22, 2025, the President inaugurated the new fourth operations building

**[00:55:20.540] AVP Narration** (asr-000517)  
of the Balingoan Port in Misamis Oriental, increasing its passenger capacity to 500 from

**[00:55:26.220] AVP Narration** (asr-000518)  
the previous 150. The President also unveiled on December 12, 2025, the new Banago Port in

**[00:55:32.880] AVP Narration** (asr-000519)  
Negros Occidental. The port can now accommodate 500 passengers from its previous 50 passengers.

**[00:55:39.360] AVP Narration** (asr-000520)  
On July 13, 2026, the President inaugurated the new

**[00:55:43.440] AVP Narration** (asr-000521)  
Benoni Ports Passenger Terminal Building and Benoni Port Lifecraft Landing Station,

**[00:55:48.720] AVP Narration** (asr-000522)  
which is seen to boost Camiguin's tourism.

**[00:55:51.520] AVP Narration** (asr-000523)  
On July 21, 2026, the President led the inspection of the new and modernized passenger terminal

**[00:55:57.600] AVP Narration** (asr-000524)  
building of the Port of Lucena. The Lucena Port PTV has increased its sitting capacity to 967

**[00:56:04.480] AVP Narration** (asr-000525)  
from its previous 607. This significantly benefits the province as the port can now

**[00:56:10.640] AVP Narration** (asr-000526)  
accommodate more tourists and passengers,

**[00:56:13.120] AVP Narration** (asr-000527)  
providing them with comfortable travel experience.

**[00:56:15.920] AVP Narration** (asr-000528)  
The PPA has also completed various port projects in different provinces in Luzon,

**[00:56:20.720] AVP Narration** (asr-000529)  
Visayas, and Mindanao. As of June 25, 2026, 87 PPA port projects are ongoing,

**[00:56:28.080] AVP Narration** (asr-000530)  
33 projects in Luzon, 33 in Visayas, and 21 in Mindanao.

**[00:56:36.390] AVP Narration** (asr-000531)  
In April 2025, the Coast Guard District in the Kalayaan Island Group was officially

**[00:56:41.350] AVP Narration** (asr-000532)  
activated to assert the country's sovereignty and enforce maritime laws.

**[00:56:45.750] AVP Narration** (asr-000533)  
The mandate of the newly activated Coast Guard District in the Kalayaan Island Group is to

**[00:56:50.150] AVP Narration** (asr-000534)  
perform maritime security, enforce Philippine maritime laws, and assert the country's

**[00:56:55.270] AVP Narration** (asr-000535)  
sovereignty in the West Philippine Sea. Meanwhile, the PCG continues to carry out

**[00:57:04.680] AVP Narration** (asr-000536)  
rescue operations and relief efforts, safeguarding millions of holiday travelers, rescuing thousands

**[00:57:11.000] AVP Narration** (asr-000537)  
of Filipinos at sea, and evacuating hundreds of thousands ahead of back-to-back Super Typhoons.

**[00:57:19.060] AVP Narration** (asr-000540)  
Beyond delivering big-ticket infrastructure projects, the DOTr remains committed to ensuring that every peso

**[00:57:24.760] AVP Narration** (asr-000541)  
of its budget is responsibly and efficiently spent for the benefit of the Filipino people.

**[00:57:30.660] AVP Narration** (asr-000542)  
The commitment was affirmed when, for the first time in over three decades, the DOTR

**[00:57:36.100] AVP Narration** (asr-000543)  
received an unmodified opinion from the Commission on Audit for Fiscal Year 2025, reflecting

**[00:57:41.920] AVP Narration** (asr-000544)  
the agency's compliance with applicable financial reporting standards and proper stewardship

**[00:57:46.840] AVP Narration** (asr-000545)  
of public funds.

**[00:57:48.000] AVP Narration** (asr-000546)  
These accomplishments in the transportation sector have opened up a broader spectrum of

**[00:57:53.320] AVP Narration** (asr-000547)  
opportunities for the country's development, helping strengthen the economy and support

**[00:57:58.140] AVP Narration** (asr-000548)  
long-term growth.

**[00:57:59.660] AVP Narration** (asr-000549)  
By addressing bottlenecks and gaps in transportation infrastructure, the DOTR remains steadfast

**[00:58:05.320] AVP Narration** (asr-000550)  
in its trust and long-term vision of improving mobility and strengthening connectivity across

**[00:58:10.420] AVP Narration** (asr-000551)  
the archipelago.

**[00:58:11.800] AVP Narration** (asr-000552)  
With several projects set for completion, the President assures Filipinos of its chief

**[00:58:16.880] AVP Narration** (asr-000553)  
goal.

**[00:58:17.260] AVP Narration** (asr-000554)  
To make their daily commute convenient and comfortable.

**[01:09:16.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000688)

**[01:09:17.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000689)

**[01:09:20.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000691)

ang

**[01:09:34.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000695)

**[01:09:37.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000696)

**[01:09:41.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000698)

**[01:09:49.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000700)

**[01:09:52.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000702)

**[01:09:58.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000703)

**[01:10:01.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000704)

**[01:10:02.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000705)

**[01:10:08.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000706)

**[01:10:09.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000707)

**[01:10:18.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000709)

**[01:10:19.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000710)

**[01:10:26.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000712)

**[01:27:41.020] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001159)  
In fact, nagkaroon kami

**[01:27:43.280] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001160)  
ng MOA with them

**[01:27:45.020] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001161)  
para magkaroon ng

**[01:27:46.880] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001162)  
suporta yung local government unit natin

**[01:27:49.160] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001163)  
sa armed forces, sa Philippine National

**[01:27:51.000] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001164)  
Police at saka sa

**[01:27:52.620] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001165)  
Philippine Coast Guard. So,

**[01:27:55.420] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001166)  
yun lang po, Secretary.

**[01:27:57.720] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001167)  
Sana po

**[01:27:58.660] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001168)  
mahanapan po natin ang paraan.

**[01:28:04.190] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001169)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[01:28:05.730] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001170)  
Tulad nga po ng ating

**[01:28:06.950] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001171)  
napag-usapan, Your Honor,

**[01:28:09.410] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001172)  
ay tama po kayo. Importante

**[01:28:11.370] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001173)  
po yung pantalan na yun.

**[01:28:13.130] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001174)  
At again,

**[01:28:14.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001175)  
kami po'y makikiusap din po

**[01:28:16.930] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001176)  
sa Kongreso kung maaari natin

**[01:28:19.190] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001177)  
maisama ito, mga ganitong mga

**[01:28:20.810] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001178)  
proyekto.

**[01:28:23.230] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001179)  
Without manifestation,

**[01:28:24.970] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001180)  
Mr. Chair, this representation

**[01:28:27.130] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001181)  
fully support na sana po

**[01:28:29.030] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001182)  
madagdagan yung budget

**[01:28:30.730] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001183)  
ng DOTR

**[01:28:32.350] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001184)  
as approved by NEP kasi

**[01:28:34.090] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001185)  
alam natin marami sa kasamahan natin dito

**[01:28:36.930] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001186)  
na yung mga locally funded nila

**[01:28:38.670] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001187)  
hindi napunduhan.

**[01:28:41.090] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001188)  
So, siguro, pwede tayo

**[01:28:43.130] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001189)  
magkaisa para matulungan

**[01:28:45.210] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001190)  
natin ang budget na

**[01:28:47.070] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001191)  
madagdagan ang DOTR po.

**[01:28:49.370] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001192)  
Yun lang po, Mr. Chair,

**[01:28:51.490] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001193)  
Secretary Lopez,

**[01:28:53.210] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001194)  
and the rest of the DTR family,

**[01:28:55.150] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-001195)  
thank you and good morning, everyone.

**[01:28:58.650] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-001196)  
Thank you very much,

**[01:29:00.050] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-001197)  
Cong Bancoro.

**[01:29:01.770] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-001198)  
Next to interpelate is

**[01:29:03.030] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-001199)  
Cong Elijah

**[01:29:05.170] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-001200)  
San Fernando.

**[01:29:06.970] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001201)  
Mr. Chair, bago po

**[01:29:09.410] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001202)  
pumatak yung oras ko,

**[01:29:11.470] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001203)  
gusto ko lang po itanong, nandito po ba yung

**[01:29:13.130] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001204)  
general manager ng MRT-3?

**[01:29:17.020] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001205)  
Mr. Chair, if I may,

**[01:29:18.600] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001206)  
I received an information po,

**[01:29:20.860] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001207)  
naka-medical leave,

**[01:29:22.740] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001208)  
and I asked specifically

**[01:29:24.120] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001209)  
kung ano nangyari sa kanya.

**[01:29:26.340] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001210)  
Baka po ko sinipo, sadyang natakot.

**[01:29:28.660] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001211)  
Ang sabi is,

**[01:29:30.400] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001212)  
cardio, eco-cardio,

**[01:29:32.120] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001213)  
pero, can I, ano,

**[01:29:34.260] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001214)  
I'll get the exact na kung anong ginagawa

**[01:29:36.280] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001215)  
sa kanya ngayon,

**[01:29:38.000] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001216)  
but it's something about eco-cardiogram,

**[01:29:41.000] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001217)  
but something about heart, siguro.

**[01:29:42.280] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001218)  
Baka po, ano.

**[01:29:44.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001219)  
Mr. Chair, gusto ko lang po i-point out,

**[01:29:45.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001220)  
kasi, nung nakaraan, nagtago na naman yan eh,

**[01:29:48.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001221)  
yung General Manager ng MRP3.

**[01:29:51.740] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001222)  
At ngayon, wala na naman.

**[01:29:54.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001223)  
Itinaon yung kanyang

**[01:29:55.300] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001224)  
general check-up kung kailan

**[01:29:56.880] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001225)  
sumasalang yung budget ng DOTR.

**[01:29:59.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001226)  
Anyway, Mr. Chair,

**[01:30:00.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001227)  
I'll proceed with my question.

**[01:30:01.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001228)  
Kaya ako po natanong kung nandito ba ang general manager ng MRT-3.

**[01:30:06.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001229)  
Kasi po noong February 3, 2026, kami po ay nagsumiti ng letter sa inyong opisina.

**[01:30:13.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001230)  
We are raising serious safety and labor concerns.

**[01:30:19.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001231)  
Yung sa safety concern, Mr. Chair, pumapatungkol po ito sa pag-bypass ng automatic train protection

**[01:30:26.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001232)  
ng MRT-3 sa North Turnback.

**[01:30:28.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001233)  
At sinasabi namin dito, Mr. Chair, na pupwedeng compromise ang safety ng mga four-car trains

**[01:30:36.820] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001234)  
na nagpa-fly-through dyan sa MRT-3.

**[01:30:40.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001235)  
Ngayon, Mr. Chair, sa halip na sagutin ito ng general manager ng MRT-3,

**[01:30:48.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001236)  
noong March 17, gumawa na ng mga retaliatory actions yung general manager ng MRT-3.

**[01:30:54.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001237)  
At nagre-reklamo po mga engineer, mga operator ng train,

**[01:30:58.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001238)  
mga expert po ng mga manager.

**[01:31:00.320] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001239)  
Instead of addressing these concerns, pinagtatanggal ni general manager kapati yung mga manggagawa na yan.

**[01:31:09.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001240)  
And in fact, sumagot sila, Mr. Chair, sa ating opisina.

**[01:31:13.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001241)  
Pero yung sagot nila, syempre, kung ang gusto naming paimbestigaan ng MRT-3,

**[01:31:17.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001242)  
syempre, sasabihin ng MRT-3, safe naman yung mga train.

**[01:31:20.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001243)  
And that is why we requested, Mr. Chair, for the OTR to come in and to investigate the matter.

**[01:31:26.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001244)  
Now, Mr. Chair, if I may recall,

**[01:31:30.320] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001245)  
During one of the lead committee hearings, nirace ko na po ito kay Secretary Banoy.

**[01:31:36.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001246)  
Alam niyo po, napakabastos niyang general manager na yan eh.

**[01:31:40.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001247)  
Kasi sinabi, wala siyang pakialam kahit congressman pa ang mag-iimbestiga sa kanya

**[01:31:44.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001248)  
dahil napakalakas niya kay Pangulong Bongbong Marcos at sa sekretary ng DOTR.

**[01:31:50.320] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001249)  
Now it has been seven months since we submitted this letter

**[01:31:53.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001250)  
and five months since sinabi niyo po, Secretary Banoy, na iimbestiga niyo yung matter.

**[01:31:58.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001251)  
Ano na po ang update dito, Secretary Banoy?

**[01:32:01.400] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001252)  
Just for update po, let me just differentiate two matters.

**[01:32:05.000] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001253)  
First on the safety issue.

**[01:32:07.700] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001254)  
Para lang po mapanatag din ang ating mga mananakay.

**[01:32:11.600] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001255)  
Yung safety issue po na sinasabi po ng mga dating empleyado ng MRT-3

**[01:32:16.260] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001256)  
were already belied by no other than three experts, foreign experts po.

**[01:32:21.740] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001257)  
Ito po yung Sumitomo, ito po yung Rylan, at saka meron pang misa eh.

**[01:32:27.220] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001258)  
Sistra.

**[01:32:27.820] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001259)  
Ito po yung talagang dalubhasa.

**[01:32:30.160] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001260)  
At sinabi nila, walang ganitong safety issue.

**[01:32:33.460] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001261)  
At is para lang mapanatag po ang ating motorista.

**[01:32:37.220] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001262)  
Kasi ang pagsasabi na hindi po ligtas ang ating riles,

**[01:32:41.840] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001263)  
ay wala po yung ano, hindi po parang sinasabi na rin natin.

**[01:32:45.980] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001264)  
It's like a bump joke po sa evasion.

**[01:32:48.740] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001265)  
Napakadelikado po niyan.

**[01:32:50.560] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001266)  
Especially now that sumasakay po ang karamihan sa ating riles.

**[01:32:55.600] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001267)  
Imagine 500,000.

**[01:32:57.820] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001268)  
Daily passengers po, no?

**[01:32:59.740] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001269)  
On the average.

**[01:33:00.800] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001270)  
At sasabihin po natin hindi ligtas.

**[01:33:03.180] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001271)  
That is quite irresponsible.

**[01:33:06.560] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001272)  
Yung sinasabi ko.

**[01:33:07.540] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001273)  
At least po, I have to be very, kumbaga, sorry po, for lack of better term.

**[01:33:13.540] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001274)  
I have to face this head on.

**[01:33:15.500] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001275)  
On the safety issue po.

**[01:33:17.120] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001276)  
Not for the OTR, but more for the commuters po.

**[01:33:20.760] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001277)  
Again, under pain of being redundant, may hindi na po the OTR ang magsasabi ho.

**[01:33:25.640] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001278)  
Baka sabihin natin self-service.

**[01:33:27.820] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001279)  
May tatlo pong dalubhasa, banyaga ho.

**[01:33:31.500] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001280)  
Na ang ginawa lang sa buong buhay nila, mag-aral po ng riles.

**[01:33:35.420] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001281)  
Sumitomo, Rylan, at sa kasistra.

**[01:33:37.800] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001282)  
Sinasabi ho, ligtas.

**[01:33:40.700] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001283)  
At least po, okay na po tayo sa aspetong yan.

**[01:33:43.260] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001284)  
Pagdating naman po kay GM Mike Kapati, tama po kayo.

**[01:33:48.000] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001285)  
Ito po ay binigay ko sa Isles, yung ating departemento.

**[01:33:52.220] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001286)  
Ininvestigan na po si GM Mike Kapati.

**[01:33:54.480] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001287)  
At hindi po po nagtatapos dyan.

**[01:33:57.100] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001288)  
Alam ko po.

**[01:33:57.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001289)  
May kaso sa Civil Service Commission.

**[01:34:01.080] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001290)  
Kasama yung I think mga iba't iba't empleyado ng MRT3.

**[01:34:05.980] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001291)  
And I can assure this committee po.

**[01:34:07.940] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001292)  
Kapag lumabas man sa resolusyon ng Civil Service Commission

**[01:34:11.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001293)  
that they violated some label provisions po.

**[01:34:15.860] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001294)  
Pagdating dito, makakaasa po kayo kung ano man yung iutos ng commission,

**[01:34:21.060] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001295)  
I'm going to implement it po.

**[01:34:22.580] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001296)  
And I'm going to furnish a copy to this committee of what we have done.

**[01:34:27.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001297)  
In the event po na mayroon na pong resolusyon.

**[01:34:30.540] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001298)  
So I'm going to defer po to the wisdom of the commission.

**[01:34:33.320] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001299)  
Kasi andyan na po yung kaso.

**[01:34:34.880] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001300)  
Tama po, lahat po tayo gobyerno.

**[01:34:36.740] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001301)  
Ang talagang ahawak po dyan is the Civil Service Commission.

**[01:34:40.320] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001302)  
Kung mayroong kailangan sa habi ng Civil Service Commission,

**[01:34:43.160] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001303)  
lahat po ay binibigay po ng aming legal.

**[01:34:45.660] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001304)  
Ang makakaasa po kayo na walang whitewash dito at wala din pong cover up.

**[01:34:49.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001305)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:34:51.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001306)  
But it has been five months po since the lead committee meeting

**[01:34:55.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001307)  
where you said that the Isles will invest in the civil service.

**[01:34:57.780] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001310)  
I would like to ask po, ano na po ba yung status doon sa Isles?

**[01:35:03.240] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001311)  
There are initial findings na po.

**[01:35:06.820] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001312)  
Ang sinabi niyo sa mga sulat niyo po kasi kagalang-galang na congressman,

**[01:35:13.540] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001313)  
yung issue po kasi ng safety and my kapati.

**[01:35:18.380] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001314)  
So yun po yung dalawang issue.

**[01:35:20.760] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001315)  
So kailangan yung dalawa ding issue na yun,

**[01:35:22.960] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001316)  
ang binigay ko sa Isles.

**[01:35:24.620] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001317)  
Not pratingnan nila.

**[01:35:26.240] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001318)  
So ito po yung talagang...

**[01:35:27.740] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001319)  
Ito po yung talagang malawakan at malalim.

**[01:35:29.700] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001320)  
Kung issue lang po sana ni my kapati,

**[01:35:31.920] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001321)  
baka mas mabilis pa tayo.

**[01:35:33.680] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001322)  
Pero sabi ko nga, may kaso sa civil service.

**[01:35:35.640] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001323)  
There's initial finding na po.

**[01:35:36.820] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001324)  
Nawala sa OSEC pa po, no?

**[01:35:38.220] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001325)  
So meron po naman kasing not protocol,

**[01:35:41.460] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001326)  
pero yan po ay dadaan pa.

**[01:35:43.060] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001327)  
Isles, legal, at rain, USEC.

**[01:35:48.200] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001328)  
I have to admit, nakita ko po yung initial findings,

**[01:35:52.380] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001329)  
deliberation po ng ating Isles.

**[01:35:54.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001330)  
But I think the more prudent...

**[01:35:55.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001331)  
But I think the more prudent...

**[01:35:57.700] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001332)  
The more prudent recourse for me,

**[01:35:59.640] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001333)  
since initial pa lang po yun,

**[01:36:01.240] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001334)  
it's not a final determination,

**[01:36:03.500] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001335)  
ay hindi ko po muna masasabi kung ano yung findings sila.

**[01:36:06.380] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001336)  
But at least I have to address already

**[01:36:08.360] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001337)  
the issue of the safety po.

**[01:36:10.500] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001338)  
Yun po kasi importante sa amin.

**[01:36:12.440] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001339)  
Thank you po.

**[01:36:13.120] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001340)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:36:15.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001341)  
Kaya ako po nire-raise itong issue na to

**[01:36:16.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001342)  
dahil kanina napag-uusapan na po natin

**[01:36:18.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001343)  
yung additional na mga station for the MRT operation.

**[01:36:22.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001344)  
At uulitin ko po, Mr. Chair,

**[01:36:25.820] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001345)  
hindi po ako papayag,

**[01:36:26.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001346)  
hahabulin ko talaga yan.

**[01:36:27.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001347)  
Si General Manager Kapati,

**[01:36:29.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001348)  
dahil napakabastos niyang General Manager na yan.

**[01:53:14.490] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001812)

**[01:53:28.350] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-001814)

**[01:53:29.190] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-001815)

**[01:53:34.850] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-001816)

**[01:53:41.190] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-001817)

**[01:53:49.650] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-001818)

**[01:53:54.770] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-001819)

**[01:53:59.190] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-001820)

**[01:54:04.130] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001821)

**[01:54:06.010] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001822)

**[01:54:12.550] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001824)

**[01:54:16.210] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001825)

**[01:54:20.130] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001826)

**[01:54:23.670] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001827)

**[01:54:25.370] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001828)

**[01:54:29.190] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001829)

**[01:54:40.510] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001830)

**[01:54:44.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001831)

**[01:54:47.510] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001832)

**[01:54:51.430] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001833)  
Magkikinabanggit po ito dito sa ginagawin natin na rails.

**[01:55:29.190] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001843)

**[01:56:25.930] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001862)
