**[01:58:23.790] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001887)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair.

**[01:58:26.370] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001888)  
Assalamualaikum warahmatullahi wabarakatuh.

**[01:58:28.910] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001889)  
Sa ating DOTR and to its attached agencies, magandang umaga po sa ating lahat.

**[01:58:36.930] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001890)  
Mr. Chair, during the DBCC briefing and in my earlier questions sa DPWH,

**[01:58:44.850] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001891)  
I already raised concerns on foreign-assisted projects, particularly on delays, penalties, and the risk of losing commitment fees.

**[01:58:53.990] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001892)  
Ngayon po, I want to extend the same line of accountability to the Department of...

**[01:58:58.910] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001893)  
Transportation.

**[01:59:00.690] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001894)  
Ilan lahat po at anong type ang mga foreign-assisted projects na ipapatupad ng DOTR this coming 2027?

**[01:59:11.350] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001895)  
On top of my mind po, per sector po, gawin natin rail, we have two, NSCR and subway.

**[01:59:20.110] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001896)  
Sa road sector natin, we have Davao.

**[01:59:23.630] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001897)  
And matatapos na po yung Cebu BRT, sa World Bank.

**[01:59:28.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001898)  
Sa maritime.

**[01:59:30.290] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001899)  
I think we have one.

**[01:59:31.450] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001900)  
Cebu with Kexim Bank.

**[01:59:34.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001901)  
And with Aviation.

**[01:59:37.650] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001902)  
Meron kami tayong NUD Dumaguete.

**[01:59:39.850] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001903)  
So more or less po mga anim po.

**[01:59:41.930] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001904)  
I might be wrong pero...

**[01:59:43.150] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001905)  
Six to seven.

**[01:59:43.970] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001906)  
Apo.

**[01:59:44.390] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001907)  
Yes.

**[01:59:44.930] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001908)  
Can you confirm also to this committee, no, Mr. Chair, that there are commitment fees paid by the taxpayers in this foreign-assisted projects na pwedeng mawala if hindi matuloy ang projects?

**[01:59:59.430] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001909)  
Tama.

**[02:00:00.000] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001910)  
Apo, we confirm po, the commitment fees is always part of the contract negotiation and the contract loan itself.

**[02:00:07.780] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001911)  
Between 2022 to present, ilan na po kaya ang projects that were placed under unprogrammed appropriations

**[02:00:16.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001912)  
and ang delayed na ang implementation or na-delay na ang implementations?

**[02:00:21.700] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001913)  
Ano-ano po kaya yung mga projects na ito?

**[02:00:23.800] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001914)  
Insofar as DOTR po is concerned, lahat po halos ng loan proceeds namin,

**[02:00:30.860] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001915)  
meron po siyang unprogrammed appropriation na component po.

**[02:00:35.840] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001916)  
Kasi po yung sa laki po ng ating foreign assisted project,

**[02:00:40.140] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001917)  
mahirapan po talaga ang ating budget naman, ang ating pondo na gagawin programmed appropriation.

**[02:00:46.560] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001918)  
Ang nangyari lang po noong manakaraang kongreso,

**[02:00:51.740] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001919)  
ay wala na po ang nailagay sa program amount, nailagay pala halos lahat sa unprogrammed appropriation.

**[02:01:00.980] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001920)  
At itong unprogrammed appropriation, nasama pa yung mga iba't ibang proyekto.

**[02:01:06.620] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001921)  
Kaya po ang nangyari, imbes bigyan prioridad yung mga foreign assisted project natin,

**[02:01:12.440] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001922)  
hindi po nabigyan ng ganoong priority at kaya po medyo napahaba,

**[02:01:17.100] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001923)  
na antala ang pagpapatupad ng proyekto at nagkaroon po,

**[02:01:21.740] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001924)  
ng mga sa malaking financing charges, suspension of works.

**[02:01:25.540] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001925)  
Kung yun po yung, I think that's the line of questioning po, Your Honor.

**[02:01:30.540] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001926)  
Yes, Mr. Chair. Actually, figures lang po, Mr. Chair, gusto ko lang malaman,

**[02:01:35.560] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001927)  
ilan yung napuntang unprogrammed appropriations natin na projects mula 2022 to present po?

**[02:01:46.020] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001928)  
Mr. Chair, may I beg the kindness indulgence of Congressman Dada.

**[02:01:52.140] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001929)  
Can we just submit po?

**[02:01:54.140] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001930)  
Para at least mas exact po yung numerong ibibigay namin, kasi importante po yung iyong katanungan.

**[02:02:00.640] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001931)  
Hopefully, within the day, we can submit po.

**[02:02:02.780] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001932)  
Yes. Maraming salamat and I am amenable po doon na you can submit that later.

**[02:02:09.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001933)  
Ayon sa COA noong 2024, ang DOTR naman daw po,

**[02:02:12.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001934)  
ang may pinakamalaking binayaran na commitment fees at around 308 million pesos.

**[02:02:19.480] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001935)  
Magkano naman po ang binayad nating commitment fees nitong 2025?

**[02:02:24.140] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001936)  
Again, Mr. Chair, when it comes to commitment fees po, the one paying is actually DOF po.

**[02:02:37.700] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001937)  
So, let us coordinate with DOF. We'll ask them po kung magkano ba yung binayaran ng DOF ng commitment fees for on behalf of DOTR po.

**[02:02:49.120] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001938)  
Then we'll get back po to the committee.

**[02:02:50.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001939)  
Yes, Mr. Chair. Actually, kaya ko ito na brought up dito at tinanong kasi kinonfirm po ng DOF during their budget,

**[02:02:58.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001940)  
hearing na meron silang around 3.4 billion pesos na binayaran from 2022 to present.

**[02:03:06.700] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001941)  
And with that, na-inform din ng DOF na ito'y sumatotal ng mga commitment fees ng under DPWH and DOTR.

**[02:03:18.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001942)  
Noong nagkaroon ng DPWH budget hearing last week, naitanong na natin ito kay Secretary Vins, Mr. Chair, at around 1.6 billion ang sinabi niya.

**[02:03:28.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001943)  
Kaya po ngayon, the same courtesy, tinatanong ko rin po sa DOTR, magkano naman po ang commitment fees under the DOTR?

**[02:03:40.970] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001944)  
Tama po, Mr. Chair. Just maybe later po, pinapatawagan ko na po yung ano kung to coordinate with DOF.

**[02:03:47.930] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001945)  
How much is the commitment fees paid by DOF for on behalf of DOTR projects po?

**[02:03:53.490] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001946)  
Sige po. For later na lang din po, to be submitted to the committee and to this representation po.

**[02:04:00.150] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001947)  
Mr. Chair, I would like to manifest.

**[02:04:02.490] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001948)  
Sa tindi ng traffic ngayon, malaking ginhawa sana sa commuters ang Metro Manila Subway Projects, EDSA Busway, Cebu Bus Rapid Transit,

**[02:04:12.830] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001949)  
pero nadidelay or nakakancel lang dahil nababagsak sa unprogrammed appropriations.

**[02:04:18.290] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001950)  
Kumbaga, ang mga taxpayers natin, naipit na nga sa traffic, nagmumulta pa ng commitment fees mula sa taxes natin.

**[02:04:26.510] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001951)  
I would like to formally request the DOTR through this committee to provide the complete data,

**[02:04:32.490] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001952)  
relevant to these questions, including projects list, timeline, and financial details.

**[02:04:38.710] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001953)  
Sana po ay ma-provide ito within the day or within the week.

**[02:04:42.290] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001954)  
Katanggap naman.

**[02:04:44.290] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001955)  
Mapunta naman po ako, Mr. Chair, sa Zamboanga Port Construction.

**[02:04:49.350] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001956)  
Maalala po natin na last year, tinerminate po ng Philippine Ports Authority ang contract with MAC Builders, Inc.

**[02:04:58.910] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001957)  
for the construction of the Zamboanga Port passenger

**[02:05:02.490] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001958)  
terminal building due to the prolonged delays.

**[02:05:06.830] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001959)  
Napenalize na po ba natin ang Mack Builders INC dahil sa delay sa completion of the project,

**[02:05:15.750] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001960)  
which ultimately lead to the termination of the contract with them?

**[02:05:24.190] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001961)  
Mr. Chair, may I defer to the general manager of PPA, please?

**[02:05:28.270] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001962)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Chair. Good morning po.

**[02:05:30.530] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001963)  
With the permission of our Secretary.

**[02:05:32.810] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001964)  
In response po dun sa tanong ni Congresswoman,

**[02:05:35.330] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001965)  
Yun po ng passenger terminal building po natin, tama po dahil nga po sa delay po no, noong completion yan,

**[02:05:43.290] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001966)  
inerminate na po natin yung kontrata po noong kontratista at ang nangyari po dyan,

**[02:05:50.690] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001967)  
ang kanila pong after assessment, ang computation po ng kanilang accomplishment is about 52%

**[02:05:56.830] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001968)  
pero ang nabayaran pa lamang po natin is 18% at hindi na po tayo nagbayad.

**[02:06:02.650] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001969)  
Yung pong balance pa po doon sa 52% at wala po tayong planong bayaran po,

**[02:06:10.310] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001970)  
your honor, yung natitira po between 80, yung pong difference pa po ng accomplishment nila sa nabayaran na po.

**[02:06:16.970] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001971)  
Kasa lukuyan po, ini-schedule po natin ang rebuilding noong passenger terminal building po natin

**[02:06:24.090] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001972)  
at dahil nga po sa nangyari po sa gitnang silangan, your honors, na tumaas po ang presyo po ng crudo,

**[02:06:32.650] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001973)  
na obliga po tayong mag-adjust po ng costo po natin doon sa mga materyalis po pati po doon sa project,

**[02:06:39.530] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001974)  
kaya po medyo natagalan po tayong konti pero inaasahan po natin na hindi po matatapos po itong taon

**[02:06:45.630] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001975)  
ay ma-re-rebeat po natin at ma-iaward at mapapatuloy na po natin yung pong completion po

**[02:06:51.750] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001976)  
noong passenger terminal building po natin sa Zamboanga City Port, your honor.

**[02:06:58.470] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001977)  
Yes, salamat po.

**[02:07:00.130] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001978)  
Given na ang Zamboanga ay isa sa pinaka-busy,

**[02:07:04.030] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001979)  
na port sa Pilipinas, can you give an update on this project kung matutuloy pa ba?

**[02:07:10.750] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001980)  
I understand na sinabi mo magkakaroon ng re-beading pero ano po yung kongkretong plano ninyo, timeline, road map?

**[02:07:20.890] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001981)  
Well, ang inaasahan po natin, your honor, is ngayon taon po, ayan po ay ma-re-rebeat,

**[02:07:26.530] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001982)  
ma-iaward at masisimulan po ang re-implementation po noong ating passenger terminal building project.

**[02:07:33.270] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001983)  
Hindi po natin maaaring abandonahin yan dahil yan po ay nasa kalagitnaan po ng ating construction

**[02:07:38.710] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001984)  
at inaasahan po natin na bago po bumaba sa ating pong Pangulo sa 2028,

**[02:07:45.170] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-001985)  
yan po ay nagagamit na po ng mga kababayan po natin sa Zamboanga pati na rin po sa Basilan at Sulu po, your honor.

**[02:07:52.430] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001986)  
Maraming salamat with that, Mr. Chair.

**[02:07:54.910] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001987)  
Finally, dadako po naman ako sa mga tanong tungkol sa maritime safety naman po.

**[02:08:01.930] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001988)  
But before that, I would like to commend Rear Admiral Atty. Oliver Tanseco, Inspector General of the PCG for conducting a thorough and swift investigation regarding the M/V Trisha Kerstin 3 incident.

**[02:08:18.550] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001989)  
And for ensuring accountability among the officers involved. Maraming salamat po.

**[02:08:23.390] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001990)  
Gayun pa man, nais ko pong i-emphasize na ang insidente ay nagsisilbing paalala at aral,

**[02:08:31.930] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001991)  
ng mga opisyal na tiyakin ang preventive measures upang maiwasan ang ganitong trahedya.

**[02:08:37.770] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001992)  
Mr. Chair, alam ko 10 seconds na lang ako pero pagpaumanhin, isang katalungan lamang po para sa marina.

**[02:08:44.170] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001993)  
Sa marina, magkano ang naka-allocate po para sa Vessel Safety Inspection for 2027?

**[02:08:51.490] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001994)  
Sapat po ba ito dahil sa dami ng registered vessel nationwide?

**[02:08:57.590] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001995)  
At kung kulang, ano naman po ang immediate plan ng marina?

**[02:09:01.930] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001996)  
Para ma-address ang gap na ito.

**[02:09:06.520] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-001997)  
Good morning to all and with the permission of the Chair and the Secretary of Transportation po.

**[02:09:14.240] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-001998)  
As to the MOOE, we can say so far enough but as we have also manifested in several hearings,

**[02:09:22.580] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-001999)  
we really need additional personnel to augment and to improve our maritime safety.

**[02:09:30.980] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-002000)  
Another item that we really also...

**[02:09:33.940] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-002001)  
also needed and we have included in our Tier 2, of course, is the continuing capacity building of our personnel considering these are very technical matters, Mr. Chair.

**[02:09:48.340] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002002)  
Mr. Chair, just a follow-up po doon.

**[02:09:50.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002003)  
So ano po yung plano ninyo now that minention ninyo na kulang yung inyong budget po for 2027?

**[02:09:57.860] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-002004)  
As to the personnel po, we have submitted na under the government approval.

**[02:10:03.940] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-002005)  
optimization plan.

**[02:10:05.820] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-002006)  
Our comprehensive proposal for manpower, of course, it will be subjected to the evaluation

**[02:10:16.100] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-002007)  
by the DBM and eventually, this will be provided to strengthen our regional offices.

**[02:10:24.780] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-002008)  
For 2027, of course, we are also appealing that other proposal under Tier 2 would be if

**[02:10:33.940] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-002009)  
it would be considered as well by the Committee on Appropriation, Mr. Chair.

**[02:10:40.340] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002010)  
Yes, Mr. Chair. With that, in closing, magbamanifest lang po ako, Mr. Chair.

**[02:10:45.980] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002011)  
I hope yung Marina magkaroon din po ng reforms undertaken

**[02:10:51.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002012)  
to ensure na walang nakakalusot ng mga vessels na hindi seaworthy

**[02:10:56.400] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002013)  
at hindi na po maulit yung nangyari sa M/V Trisha Kerstin 3.

**[02:11:00.500] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002014)  
Mr. Chair, nais ko pong imanifest din na patuloy kong imomonitor

**[02:11:07.180] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002015)  
at patuloy pong imomonitor ng Akbayan Reform Block

**[02:11:10.940] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002016)  
ang performance at budget utilization ng Marina,

**[02:11:14.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002017)  
lalo na pagdating sa vessel safety, inspections, at compliance sa international conventions.

**[02:11:21.460] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002018)  
Bilang pangunahing ahensya na nag-oversee ng maritime industry,

**[02:11:25.740] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002019)  
tungkulin ng Marina na siguraduhin na wala nang mangyayaring trahedya

**[02:11:30.480] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002020)  
gaya po ng nangyari sa M/V Trisha Kerstin 3.

**[02:11:33.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002021)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair.

**[02:11:35.960] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-002022)  
Thank you very much, Cong Dada.

**[02:11:38.520] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-002023)  
And I would like to acknowledge the presence of Representative Chan.

**[02:11:44.820] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-002024)  
And then next to interpolate is from the City of Golden Friendship,

**[02:11:49.120] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-002025)  
the most young-looking, Rufus Rodriguez.

**[02:11:51.920] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002026)  
Thank you very much, Co-Chair Ray Arogancia, my confadre, Chair Erwin Ongchuan.

**[02:12:00.480] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002027)  
And my distinguished colleagues, especially the Chairman of the Committee on Transportation,

**[02:12:04.660] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002028)  
Franz Pumaren.

**[02:12:06.620] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002029)  
Mr. Chairman, I heard the opening statement of our dear Secretary,

**[02:12:12.260] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002030)  
Secretary Lopez, and heard the presentation of [unclear],

**[02:12:16.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002031)  
and saw that video presentation,

**[02:12:18.840] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002032)  
and we can see the outstanding achievements of the Department of Transportation

**[02:12:24.600] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002033)  
and the attached agencies on land, on sea, and air.

**[02:12:29.800] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002034)  
And I remember,

**[02:12:30.480] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002035)  
I remember Winston Churchill in 1942 fighting the Nazi Germany.

**[02:12:36.320] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002036)  
And he said in his address to the Parliament of Great Britain, he said,

**[02:12:42.360] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002037)  
we shall fight them on the land, we shall fight them in the sea,

**[02:12:49.260] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002038)  
we shall fight them on the air until Nazi Germany will perish from this earth.

**[02:12:56.260] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002039)  
You see, the D.O.T.R.

**[02:12:59.220] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002040)  
Achievements Railway,

**[02:13:02.640] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002041)  
bus traffic system, bus transit.

**[02:13:06.540] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002042)  
That is land, sea.

**[02:13:08.480] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002043)  
You see the ports and everything.

**[02:13:12.160] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002044)  
And then the D.C.A., our air, the airports.

**[02:13:15.920] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002045)  
And then, of course, we have here the Philippine Coast Guard fighting it out

**[02:13:21.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002046)  
that China will never get an inch, as President Marcos has said,

**[02:13:26.120] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002047)  
an inch of Philippine territory.

**[02:13:28.060] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002048)  
So with that, Mr. Chairman,

**[02:13:29.220] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002049)  
I am therefore constrained in the proper time

**[02:13:32.680] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002050)  
to move for the approval of the budget of the D.O.T.R.

**[02:13:38.500] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002051)  
plus maraming projects na hindi na-funded.

**[02:13:42.720] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002052)  
And I'd like to therefore propose to the plenary

**[02:13:45.640] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002053)  
that we give additional 30 billion,

**[02:13:49.440] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002054)  
or the 300 billion,

**[02:13:50.800] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002055)  
so that a lump sum,

**[02:13:55.040] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002056)  
a lump sum of 30 billion

**[02:13:57.040] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002057)  
so that through the leadership of Banoy Lopez,

**[02:14:00.540] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002058)  
we are able to also proceed with the implementation of the other projects

**[02:14:07.620] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002059)  
as determined by the D.O.T.R. and the attached agencies

**[02:14:11.040] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002060)  
to be very important for the development of the nation.

**[02:14:14.000] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002061)  
Indeed, the Department of Transportation is a vital cog, Mr. Chairman,

**[02:14:19.020] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002062)  
in the development of our country

**[02:14:20.440] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002063)  
so that we have more economic development, social activity, and mobility.

**[02:14:26.160] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002064)  
And that is why, on the proper time, I will do that.

**[02:14:28.980] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002065)  
But I have some questions.

**[02:14:30.540] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002066)  
To the D.O.T.R.

**[02:14:32.820] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002067)  
Just three short ones.

**[02:14:34.320] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002068)  
First question.

**[02:14:36.340] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002069)  
Can you explain, because my residents in Cagayan,

**[02:14:39.540] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002070)  
my constituents,

**[02:14:40.820] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002071)  
are now listening to us,

**[02:14:42.660] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002072)  
what are the steps you have taken

**[02:14:44.220] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002073)  
for the Mindanao Railway System 3

**[02:14:47.420] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002074)  
from Villanueva, Tagoloan, Misamis Oriental,

**[02:14:52.560] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002075)  
to Cagayan de Oro,

**[02:14:54.060] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002076)  
and then to Lagindingan?

**[02:14:55.480] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002077)  
Can you tell us, Secretary?

**[02:14:57.260] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002078)  
Because we have talked to each other in the consultation.

**[02:15:00.000] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002079)  
Very good news. I'd like our people of Cagayan de Oro to see you and listen to you on what is the move forward so that we will have the railway in Region 10.

**[02:15:13.360] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002080)  
This is the Railway Phase 3. Thank you.

**[02:15:17.940] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-002081)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. First, we are humbled by your kind words, Your Honor.

**[02:15:22.140] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-002082)  
Para sagutin po ang katanungan, Mr. Chair, tama po kayo. Isa po kayo sa talagang tumutulak sa Mindanao Rail Project natin, hindi lang sa Phase 1, Phase 2, at sinasabi nating Phase 3.

**[02:15:35.580] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-002083)  
Dahil po sa inyong pagtutulak, at talaga namang importante ito, ang sinabi nga po namin noong una ninyo itong ni-raise sa amin last year ay magsisimula na kami ng aming pre-feasibility.

**[02:15:48.300] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-002084)  
Natapos na po yung pre-feasibility namin ngayon. Papunta po kami sa feasibility study.

**[02:15:52.140] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-002085)  
At kukuha kami sa pondo ng sinasabi nating PPDMF.

**[02:16:00.060] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-002086)  
Gayon pa man, for the entire Mindanao Rail po, as committed po sa inyo last budget hearing, patuloy pa rin po kami nanliligaw ng magpopondo po dito sa napakalaking proyekto.

**[02:16:15.520] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-002087)  
As a matter of fact po, next week po, mayroon pong kasama ang ating mahal na Pangulo.
